Cheques Service ^KYUlXU4e

Requirements ^WEUbM2Bd


Cheque Deposit

1. Allow a customer to scan a cheque on the mobile device
2. Send cheque + payment details to ClearBank (who process the cheque & deposit the funds)
3. Inform customer whether the cheque has been paid or not
4. Ensure data about the cheque deposit (and all other cheque deposits) is available for querying by other internal services ^OQ6rgVuq

mobile device ^iHpDAM3a

ClearBank ^jJlRIO9c

Secondary Requirements

1. Maintain full audit trail ^nq1JJclJ

- Propose microservice boundaries
    
- Design APIs
    
- Propose event flows and data models
    
- Discuss reliability, scalability, and operational considerations ^c1y5vPJN
